Explanatory Remarks:

1. No cleaning-up process is applied.

Text:

āyurvedīyagranthamālā ||
upādhyāyaśrīmādhaviracita
mathamo'dhyāyaḥ
arthaprakāśakāsāravilaśomṣujinīmaryama |
saccidānandavibhavaṃ śivayorvapurāśraye || 1 ||
devendramaulimandaramakāndakaṇāruṇāḥ |
veinnaṃ hṛrantu herambacaraṇāmbujareṇavaḥ || 2 ||
adhunā rasarājakha saskārān saipracakṣmahe |
nānātantrāṇi seṃdṛśya bhiṣajāṃ siddhihetave || 3 ||
saṃskārāḥ paratatreṣṭhaṃ ye gūḍhāḥ siddhasūcitāḥ|
tāneva prakaṭīkartumudyamaṃ kila kurmahe || 4 ||
granthādasmādāharanti prayogān khīyaṃ bām̐'sinnāmadheyaṃ kṣipanti|
gotrāṇyeṣāmasadīyaśramoṣmā masīkurvannāyurga bobhavītu || 5 ||
am̐śraurṣa bahuviduṣā mukhādapaśyaṃśākheṣu sthitamakṛtaṃ na tallikhāmi||
yatkarma vyaracayamagrato gurūṇāpraiauḍhānā tadiha vadāmivīrtanam̐kaḥ||
adhyāpayanti yadvaii dayituṃ kṣamante
srutendrakamai gukho guravasta eva |
śiṣyāsta eva racayati puro gurūṇa
śeṣāḥ punastadubhayābhinayaṃ bhajante || 7 ||
1 *vibhavāmbuñjinīmayaṃ khaca || 2 *siddhisūcitā * khaca || 3 লঙ্কিলন ye nikṣipanti' kha' || 4*ākhādya' ka. || 5*vistareṇa' ka |
yadyanmayā'kriyata kārathituṃ ca śakyaṃ
sūtendrakarma tadih prathayābabhūve |
adhyāpayanti ya idaṃ na tu kārayanti
kurvanti nedamadhiyantyubhaye mṛṣārthoḥ || 8 ||
saskārāḥ śivajanurṣo bahuprakārā
stulyā ye laghubahulamayāsasādhyāḥ |
yadyekaṃ sukaramudāharāmi teṣāṃ
vyāhṛraiiḥ kimih phala tataḥ pareṣām || 9||
karma yadyadasādhyaṃ khāḍurlabhaṃ yadyadauṣadham |
tattatsarvaṃ parityajya sārabhūtaṃ traikāśitam || 10 ||
kacicchākhe kriyā nāsti kairmasaṃkhyā na ca kacin |
rasayuktiḥ kracinnāsti saṃpradāyaḥ kaścinna ca || 11 ||
am̐taḥ siddhirna sarvatra rase vāpi rasāyane |
vaidyavāde prayoge ca tasmādyatno mayā kṛtaḥ || 12 ||
yadyadgurumukhodbhīta khānubhūtaṃ ca yanmayā |
tattaleokahṛitārthīya vakṣyāmyanaitivirūtaram || 13 ||
yadanyatra tadtrāsti yadatrāsti na kurtracit |
muktvaikaṃ rasuvaidyaṃ tu lābhaṃ pūjā ca kīrtenam |
tṛṇakāṣṭhauṣadhairvaidyaḥ ko labhetaṃ varāṭikām || 14 ||
yāvatsūto na śuddho naca mṛta athavā mūrcchito gandhabaddho
no vajṛṃ māritaṃ syānna ca gaganavadho nopasrutāśca śuddhāḥ |
khaṇodya sūtrailohaṃ viṣmapi nū ghṛtaṃ tailapāko na jāta
stāvadvaidyaḥ k siddho bhavati vasubhrujā maṇḍale ślāghanīyaḥ |

1‘samuddhṛtam ka | 2 ‘kramasakhyā' ka | 3 tena siddhirna tatrāsti' ka | 4 ‘mukhāt jñāta' ka || 5 ‘prakṭīkriyate'dhunā' ka || 6 ‘tat kcit' ka
atha rasadoṣāḥ |

nāgo vaṅgo malo vahniśvāpalyaṃ ca veiṣa giriḥ |
asahyāgneirmahrādoṣā nisargātpārade sthitāḥ || 16 ||
jāḍayagaṇḍauṃ tanau nāgāt kuṣṭha vaṅgādujo malāt |
dāho vahevīryanāśaśvāpalyānmaraṇaṃ viṣāt || 17 ||
gireḥ sphoṭo'pyasahṛtādoṣānmoho'bhijāyate |
veiṣa vahnirmalaśceti doṣā mukhyatamāstrayaḥ || 18 ||
rase maraṇasaṃtāpamūcchanā hetavaḥ kramāt |
yogikau nāgavaṅgau dvau tau jāḍyādhmānakuṣṭhadau ||19||
aupādhikāḥ punaścānye kīrtitāḥ sapta kaṃcukāḥ |
bhūmijo girijo vājoṃ dvau ca dvau nāgavaṅgajau || 20 ||
dvādaśaite rase doṣāḥ proktā rasaviśāradaiḥ |
bhūmijaḥ kurute kuṣṭha girijo jāḍyameva ca || 21 ||
vārijo vātasaṃghāta doṣāḍhyau nāgavaṅgajau |
ato doṣanivṛttyarthaṃ rasaḥ śodhyaḥ prayatnataḥ || 22 ||
śodhito rasarājastu sudhātulyaphalapradaḥ |
doṣahīno yadā sūtastadā mṛtyujarāpahaḥ || 23 ||
sākṣādamṛtamapyeṣa doṣayuktī raso viṣam |
tasmāddoṣanivṛttyarthaṃ sahrāyairnipuṇebhaiiṣak || 24 ||
sarvopaskaramādāya rasakarma samācaret |
raso grāhyaḥ śubhe kāle palānā śatamātrakam || 25 ||
pañcāśatpañcarviśadvā daśa paṃcaikameva ca |
palādūne na kartavyo rase saṃskāra uttamaḥ || 26 ||
bahuprayāsasādhyatvātphala khalrpa yato bhavet|
saṃpūjya śrīguruṃ kanyāṃ baṭukaṃ ca gaṇādhipam || 27 ||

yoginī kṣetrapālāśva caturdhā balipūrvakam|
tato rahakhanilaye sumuhūrte vidhorbale || 28 ||
sudine śubhanakṣatre rasaśodhanamācerat |
aghoreṇa ca marceṇa rarsa prakṣālya pūjya ca |
khedanādimukhān paścātsaṃskārān samyagācaret || 29 ||
‘aghorebhyo'tha ghorebhya' ityādi vaideiko mantraḥ |
khalvo lohamayaḥ śreṣṭhastasmācchreṣṭhastu sārajaḥ |
kāntalohabhavastakhāt, madaikaśca tathāvidhaḥ || 30 ||
abhāve lohakhalvasya srigdhapāṣāṇajaḥ śubhaḥ |
tādṛśakhacchamasṛṇamardakena samanvitaḥ || 31 ||
aṣṭādaśaiva saṃskārā ūnaviṃśatikāḥ kacit |
saṃgreoktā rasarājasya vasusaṃkhyā: kacinmatāḥ || 32 ||
yathā-khātkhedanaṃ tadanu mardanamūrcchanaṃ ca
syādusthiti: patanarodhaniyāmanāni |
saṃdīpanaṃ gaganabhakṣṇamānamatra
saṃcāraṇaṃ tadanu garbhagatā dutiśva || 33 ||
bāhyā dutiḥ sūtakajāraṇā khādrāgastathā sāraṇakarma paścāt |
saṃkrāmaṇaṃ vedhavidhiḥ śarīrayogastathā'ṣṭādṛśadhā'tra karme ||34||
rasapaddhalyā---
khedo madenamūrchanotthiti tataḥ prāto'pi bhedānvito
rodhaḥ saṃyamanaṃ śraddīpanamiti spaṣṭā'ṣṭadhā saiṃskṛtiḥ |
akhāḥ sarvarasopayogikatayā tvanyā na vinyakhate
granthe'smin makṛtopayogavirahrādvistārabhīlyā'thavā ||35||
vāgmaṭo'pi---
ityaṣṭau sūtasaṃskārāḥ samā dravye rasāyane |
śeṣā dravyopayogitvānna te vaidyopayobhinaḥ || 36 ||

athonaviṃśatikamīṇi |
yathā-khedanamardanamūnchainotthāpanapātūnabodhananiyamanasaṃdīpanānuvāsanagaganādigrāsapramāṇacāraṇagabhaiduti-bāhyaduti-yogajāraṇarañjanasāraṇakrāmaṇavedhanabhakṣṇākhyā ūnaviṃśatisaṃskārāḥ sūtasiddhidā bhavanti; dīpanāntā aṣṭī saṃskārā vā dehasiddhidā bhavanti |
atha teṣu khednavidhi: |--
nānāvānyaiyethāprānaistuṣavarjerjalānvitaiḥ |
sruñjhāṇḍa pūritaṃ rakṣedyāvadamlatvamāmuyāt || 37 ||
tanmadhye ghanavāyuṇḍīviṣṇukrāntāpunarnavāḥ |
mīnākṣī caiva sapakṣī sahadevī śatāvarī || 38 ||
triphalā girikarṇī ca haṃsapādī ca citraka: |
saimūlakāṇḍaṃ piṣṭhā tu yathālābhaṃ vinikṣipet || 39 || pūrvīmlabhāṇḍamadhye tu dhānyāmlakamidaṃ bhavet |
khedanādiṣu sarvatra rasarājakha yojayet|
atyamlamāranālaṃ vā tadabhāve prayojayet || 40 ||
ghano mustā, vāk brāhmī, kecit 'tanmadhye bhaḍurāmuṇḍī' iti paṭhanti, muṇḍī prasiddhā, viṣṇukrāntā śvetapuṣpā aparājitā, punarnavā gandhapūrṇeti bhāṣā, matsyākṣī mīnākṣī 'machechī' iti bhāṣā, (hum̐nagudā ityapi) sarpākṣī sarpanetropamā ‘cucī' iti bhāṣā, sahadevī prasiddhā, śatāvarī śatāvarīti bhāṣā, triphalā prasiddhā, girikarṇī nīlapuṣpā aparājitaiva, iṃsapādī ham̐sacaraṇā,. citrakaḥ prasiddhaḥ | samūlakāṇḍaṃ samūlaśākhaṃ, yathālābha ardhasaṃkhyādhikamiti gurusaṅketaḥ ||

1 samūlakāni piṣṭāni' ka 2 'ulagundā' kha.
tryūṣaṇaṃ lavaṇa rājī laśunaṃ triphalā''rdrakam |
mahobalā nāgabalā meghanādaḥ punarnavā || 41 ||
meṣaśṛṅgī citrakaśca navasāraḥ samaṃ samam |
rasasya ṣoḍaśāṃśena sarve yuñjyāt pṛthak pṛthak || 42 ||
etatsamasta vyastai vā pūrvāmlenaiva peṣayat |
śralimpettena kalkena vastra cāṅgulimātrakam || 43 ||
tanmadhye nikṣipetsūta baddhā tatridinaṃ pacet ||
dolāyatre'mlasaṃyute jāyate khedito rasaḥ || 44 ||
meghanādastaṇḍulīyabheda: patraśākaviśeṣaḥ ‘cavalāī' iti bhāṣā, śrṛṅgī meṣaśṛṅgī tadalābhe karkaṭaśrṛṅgī grāhmā, naksāro navasāgaraḥ kṣāraviśeṣaḥ |
athānyasiddhamatam --
tryūṣaṇaṃ lavaṇāsuyoṃ citrakārdrakamūlakam |
piṣṭhā sruto muhuḥ khedyaḥ kāñjikena dinatrayam || 45 ||
tryūṣaṇaṃ trikuṭu, lavaṇaṃ saindhavaṃ, āāsurī rāī, citrakaḥ śrasiddhaḥ,ārdraka śṛṅgaveraṃ, mūlakaṃ śvetamūlakaḥ ‘mulī' iti bhāṣā | ṣṭriā tatkalena varkha liptvetyādi pūrvavat | muhuriti pratidinaṃ kalkāṭṭattirityarthaḥ | athānyasiddhamatam -
dirna vyoṣavarāvahvikanyākalkeṣu kāñcike |
rasaṃ caturguṇe vasne baddhā dolākṛtaṃ pacet || 46 ||
vyoṣaṃ trikaṭu, varā triphalā, vahniśvitraka:; kanyā ‘kuvārī' iti bhāṣā, tanmadhyagarbho grāhyaḥ |
anyañca-
rasasya ṣoḍaśāśena dravyaṃ yuñjyātpṛthak pṛthak |
dravyeṣvanuktamāneṣu mataṃ mānamidaṃ budhaiḥ || 47 ||

trideina khedanāśaktau dinamekaṃ nirantaram |
khedayedrasarājaṃ tu nātitīkṣṇena vahninā || 48 ||
kecitu mardanapūrvakaṃ khedanamicchanti, te upapatijñānarahitāḥ, kramavirodhaśca teṣāṃ (mate) dūṣaṇam |
atha mardanam |
bhiṣagvimardayecūrṇaimaiilitvā ṣoḍaśāṃśataḥ |
srutasya gālitairveśtrairvakṣyamāṇadravādibhiḥ || 49 ||
gṛhadhūmeṣṭikācūrṇa tathā dadhiguḍānvṛitam |
lavaṇosurisayukta kṣiptvā srutaṃ vimardayet || 50 ||
jīṇībhrakaṃ tathā jīrṇa bījaṃ sūtastathaiva ca |
gṛhṇāti nimelo rāgān grāse grāsa vimarditaḥ || 51 ||
athānyasiddhamatam -
rakteṣṭikāniśādhūmasāroṇībhakhacūrṇakaiḥ |
jambīradravasaṃyutairmuhumedyoṃ dinatrayam || 52 ||
dinaika vāpi sūtaḥ khānmardanānnirmala: param |
ūrdhavepātanayaṃtreṇa gṛhṇīyāca punaḥ punaḥ || 53 ||
paṭasāraṇato vā'pi kṣālanādāranālataḥ ||
atha mūcchainam |
gṛhkanyā malaṃ hṛnti triphalā vahnināśinī || 54 ||
citramūlaṃ viṣaṃ hṛnti tasmādabhiḥ prayatnataḥ |
miśritaṃ mardayetstaṃ saptavārāṇi mūrcchayet || 55 ||
itthaṃ samūrcchitaḥ sruto doṣaśūnyaḥ prajāyate |
savārāṇi mūccheyediti dhātuvāde, na raseṣu || 56 ||
athānyanmatam| |---
rāja़dṛkṣukha mūlenū marduyetsaha kanyayā |
maladoṣāpanuttyurtha mardanotthāpane śubhe || 57 ||

kṛṣṇadhatūrakadravaiśvāñcalyavinivṛttaye |

giridoṣa trikaṭunā kanyātīyena yatnataḥ || 59 ||

āranālena coṣṇena pratidoṣa viśodhayet || 60 ||
eva saṃśodhitaḥ srutaḥ saptadoṣavivarjitaḥ |
jāyate kāyekartā ca anyathā kāryenāśakaḥ || 61 ||
atha rasasya kakṣukaharaṇam |
kumārikācitrukuraktasarṣapaiḥ

iti yugapatsamalanāśanam ||

vāgbhaṭo'pi-

prakṣālya kākhikai sāmlaikhamādāya vimardayet|

iti lugamaprakāraḥ |

atha pātanam |
taca trividhaṃ—ūrdhvādhastiryagbhedāt; tatroupātanaṃ yathā-
bhāgāstrayo rasakhā'rkecūrṇeśyaiko'tha nimbukaiḥ || 68 ||
etatsaṃmardayettāvadyāvadāyati piṇḍatām |
tatpiṇḍaṃ talabhāṇḍasthamūrdhvabhāṇḍe jalaṃ kṣipet || 69 ||
kṛtvā''lavāla kenāpi dattvā cārdra hi plotakam |
saṃmudrayāgnimadhastakha caturyāmaṃ prabodhayet || 70 ||
yuktyodhrvabhāṇḍasaṃlagraṃ gṛhṇīyātpāradaṃ tataḥ |

sasūtabhāṇḍavivaramanyuddhilati bhāṇḍakam |

rasasya mānāniyamātkathituṃ naiva śakyate | 73 ||
akecūirṇeya tāmrarajasaḥ ||
athānyanmatam -
mayūragrīvatāpyābhyāṃ naṣṭapiṣṭīkṛtakha ca |
yace ḍamaruke kuryādrasendrakhodhrvapātanam || 74 ||
mayūragrīvaṃ tutthakaṃ, tāpyaṃ kharṇamākṣirka, naṣṭapiṣṭāṃkṛtakha
‘kumārikādravayogena' iti śeṣaḥ ||
athādhaḥpātanam |
triphalāśiyuśikhibhirlavaṇāmūriseṃyutaiḥ |
naṣṭapiṣṭa rasaṃ kṛtvā lepayeddhrvabhāṇḍake || 75 ||

ūrdhavebhāṇḍodaraṃ lipvā'dhobhāṇḍe jalaṃ kṣipet |
sandhilepaṃ dvayoḥ kṛtvā tadyatra bhruvi pūrayet || 76 ||
upariṣṭātpuṭe datte jale patati pāradaḥ |
adhaḥpātanamityuktaṃ siddhādyaiḥ srutakarmeṇi || 77 ||
triphaleti vyākhyātā | adhobhāṇḍe jalaṃ kṣipediti vacanātsaṃpuṭapātramadhye jala prāpyate, tacca saṃpradāyaviruddhaṃ, ataḥ saṃpūrṇāmbuni pātrāntare bhūmau nikhātite takhopari sapuṭa dadyāt, paścāttakha saṃpuṭakhādhaḥpātrasya ca saṃdhirodhaḥ kārya iti tātparyeto jñeyam | upariṣṭātpuṭe datte iti puṭaṃ cātra keaukuṭa yathāyatharasamātrāpekṣayā vā viśativanotpalairadhikaivī nivātasthale śanaiḥ śanairdeyamiti gurusaśradāyaḥ ||
athānyanmatam -
navanītābhrakaṃ sūta ghṛṣṭvā jambhāmbhasā dinam |
vānarīśigruśikhibhirlavaṇāsurisaṃyutaiḥ || 78 ||
‘naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ rasaṃ kṛtvā' ityādi pūrvavat |
atha tiryakpātanam |
ghaṭe rasaṃ vinikṣipya sajalaṃ ghaṭamanyakam |
tiryabukhaṃ dvayoḥ kṛtvā samukha rodhayetsudhīḥ || 79 ||
tathaiva cullyā saṃsthāpya yatnatastu tato bhiṣak |
rasādho jvālayedagniṃ yāvatsruto jalaṃ veiśet 80 ||
tiryakpātanamityuktaṃ siddhainīgārjunādibhiḥ |
miśritau cennāgavaṅgau rase vikrayahetunā || 81 ||
tābhyāṃ syāt kṛtrimo doṣastanmuktiḥ pātanatrayāt |
evaṃ susaṃskṛtaḥ.chtaḥ pātnāvadhi yatnataḥ || 82 ||
sarvadoṣavinirmukto jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 83 ||

atrāvasare kecitpunaḥ khedanamicchanti; yathā–

yathā-
viśvāmitrakapāle vā kācakūpyāmathāpi vā |
sraṣṭyambujaṃ vinikṣipya tatra tanmajjanāvadhi || 88 ||
pūyetridinaṃ bhūmyā rājahrutapramāṇataḥ |
anena srutarājo'yaṃ ṣaṇḍhabhāva vimuñcati || 89 ||
viśvāmitrakapālaṃ nārikelapātraṃ, ‘sṛṣṭyamburja saindhavavāri' ityeke, apare tu ‘mūtra rajaḥ śukra ca' ityāhuḥ | yukte tu sṛṣṭyambujaṃ arugṇāyāḥ ṣoḍaśavārṣikyāḥ striya ātevam | rājahrutaḥ sapādakaraḥ |
matāntaraṃ sugama ca
kūdurthananaiva napuṃsakūtvamevaṃ bhvedakha rasakha padhūt |

1 "pātairmanda śānto' kha !

matāntaram--
lavaṇenāmlapiṣṭena haṇḍikāntargataṃ rasam |
ācchādyātha jalaṃ kiṃcit kṣiptvā śrāveṇa rodhayet ||91||
ūrdhvaṃ laghupuṭo deyaḥ prāptapuṃstvo raso bhavet || 92 ||
matāntaram-
eva kadarthitaḥ srutaḥ ṣaṇḍhabhāvaṃ prayāti hi |
bahvauṣadhikaṣāyeṇa kheditaḥ sabalo bhavet || 93 ||
yathā-
sarpākṣīciñcikāvandhyābhṛṅgābdaiḥ khedito balī |
nirastaṣaṇḍhabhāvo'sau jāyate hi rasottamaḥ || 94 ||
sarpokṣī nāgiṇī, ciñcā amlikā, vandhyā vājhakhakhasā, bhṛṅgo bhṛṅgarājaḥ, abdo mustā nāgarasaṃjñako grāhyaḥ | eṣā raso grāhyaḥ |

sarpākṣī ciñcikā vanyābhṛṅgābdakanakāmbubhiḥ |
dinaṃ saṃkheditaḥ sruto niyamāt sthiratāṃ vrajet || 95 ||
matāntaram-
uttarāśābhavasthūlaraktasaindhavaloṣṭaka. |
tadgarbhe randhrarka kṛtvā sūtaṃ tatra vinikṣipet || 96 ||
tatastu caṇakakṣārra dattvā copari nimbujam|
rasuṃ prakṣipya dātavyaṃ tāiksaindhavakhuṇḍakam||17||
gataṃ kṛtvā dharāgabha dattvā saṃndhavasapuṭam |
limaṣṭāṅgulāṃ dattvā kārīṣaṃ dinasakam || 98 ||
vṛddhiṃ prajvālva tadvāhya kṣālayetkāñjikena ca |
ath niyamano nāma saṃskāro gadvilo budhaiḥ || 99 ||

abhāve caṇakakṣārakhārpayennavasāgaram |
kharjikā vā pradātavyā nūnamityāha bhāskaraḥ || 100 ||

athavā citrakadrāvai: kāñjike tridinaṃ pacet |
dīpanaṃ jāyate takha rasarājasya cottamam || 103 ||
athānuvāsanam |

dinaika dhārayeddharma mṛtpātre vā śilodbhave || 104 ||
ityanuvāsanam |
sahasranimbūphalatoyaghṛṣṭo raso bhavedvahnisamaprabhāvaḥ |

iti rasacintāmaṇāvanuvāsanam ||
etairanuvāsanāntairnavabhiḥ saṃskāraiḥ śuddhaḥ sruto'ṣṭamāśa avaśiṣyata ityāha rasaratnākarakṛnnityanāthaḥ—
khedanādinavakarmasaskṛtaḥ satkañcukavivarjito bhavetū |
aṣṭamāśa avaśiṣyate tadā śuddhasruta iti kathyate budhaiḥ || iti ||

śuddhasrutaparīkṣayamiti bhāvaḥ| kīdṛśaḥ khedanādinabakarmabhiḥ saskṛtaḥ pāradaḥ satkañcukavivarjito bhavedatra dṛṣṭapralyaya māha–(kukhukavivarjitakheya parīkṣā) yadā aṣṭamāśa avaśi ṣyate, saptabhāgāḥ saptakañcukasaṃbandhino gacchantyaṣṭamo bhāgaḥ pāradakhāvaśiṣyate tadā śuddhasūtaḥ kañcukadoṣavinirmukto 2

bhavati | saptaḥkañcukāḥ saptāvaraṇāni śivaśāpājjātāni, tadvimuktatayā śuddhasruto budhaiḥ kathyata iti | atha jāraṇam | jāraṇā hi nāma gālanapātanavyatirekeṇa ghanahemādigrāsapūrvakapūrvāvasthāpratipannatvaṃ, phalaṃ cāsya prokte khaya maheśvareṇa---

sarvapāpakṣaye jāte prāpyate rasajāraṇā |
tatprāptī prāptameva syāṣṭrijñāna muktilakṣaṇam |

mardanaṃ candanaṃ caiva grāsaḥ pūjā'bhidhīyate || 108 ||

dravanti tasya pāpāni kurvannapi na lipyate || 110 ||
ityādi ||

anyatrāpi –

"ṣaḍguṇe rogaghna' ityuktaṃ yattatu mūrchāyāmevādhigantavya, tatra gandhakasya samagrajāraṇāyā abhāvāt | atra tu same gandhe iti tṛtīyārthe saptamī; ( tena samena gandhena tulyagandhena jīṇaiḥ pārado mūcchainābidhinā mṛtaḥ, tatphala rogaptatā ) evaṃ dviguṇādiṣvapi vyākhyeyamiti pratibhāti |

1 ‘sarveṣāṇḍhyavināśana' ka | 2 ‘bhavet kha | 3 aya pāṭha kha pustake nopalabhyate || 4 ‘syājjāraṇāvahnistathā' ka |
athādau gandhakajāraṇam |

tatu tantranigadeitadevatāparicaraṇasamanantaraṃ tattacchodhanaśrakriyābhirbahvībhiḥ saṃkṣiptābhivī śuddhasya rasendrasya tṛṇārarṇimaṇijanyavahninyāyena tāratamyamavalokamānaiḥ sūkṣmamatibhiḥ ‘palārdhenāpi saṃskāraḥ kartavyaḥ sūtakasya ca' iti rasārṇavavacanābdyāvahārikatolakadvayapramāṇenāpi kaścana sūtaṃ samādāya, tatra gandhakī navanītākhyaḥ saṃśodhya jāye āāṣaṅguṇa miti | tato māraṇavidhinā hṛtvā mātrayā'śita: pathyayogena ‘same gandhe tu rogaghna' ityādiproktaphalakārī raso bhavati | atha yadi rasāyanaguṇecchā cedbhandhakajāraṇottaraṃ hemābhrasattvādei yathāvibhava gandhakajāraṇotthite rase śroktavidhinā sasām̐dhya jāyeṃ māraṇavidhinā hatvā mātrayā kṣetrīkaraṇapūrvaka pathryayogena maṇḍalāvadhi ṣaṇmārsa varṣaka dvivarṣa trivarṣa vā sasevyaḥ | tadā taduktaphalamāpnoti | yāvañjīvaṃ sevyo vā saṃpattiścet | yadvā yathāvakāśā paunaḥpunyena saṃsevyaḥ | atha yadi kevala rogannecchā cetkhedanādidīpanāntaiḥ saskarai rarsa saśodhya proktavidhinā gandhakena samūccharya mātrayā pathryayogena saṃsevyo yāvadārogyamiti | atha rasādī pākāvalehaguṭikācūrṇādau vā rasayoge vihite yathāvacana saṃśuddho mūrcchito gandhajoṇe hemādijīrṇo vā mṛto'mṛto vā raso yojya iti gurusaṅketaḥ | gandhakajāraṇarahitaḥ seśuddho'pi raso yogeṣu na yojyaḥ, gadhntṛtvaśaktyanudayāt | hemādijīrṇo'pyadyuddhastu kutrāpi na yojya, vaiguṇyapradatvāt | eva sarva uparasā dhātava upadhātavo ratnānyuparatnāni viṣopaviṣaprabhṛtayaśca

1‘taraṇimaṇi- ka || 2 ‘saśuddha' kha' || 3 catvāriṃśaddinaparyantamityarthaḥ | śuddhāḥ santo mṛtā amṛtā vā yathāvacanaṃ yojyā nāśuddhā iti dik|| iha khalu puruṣeṇa duḥkhakha nirupādhidveṣaviṣayatvāttadabhāvaścikīrṣitavyo bhavati, sukhamapi nirupādhipremāspadatayā gaveṣaṇīrya, tadetatpuruṣārthadvayam| atra srakcandanalalanādiviṣayāṇā salūpi tatkāraṇatve(sukhūkāraṇale)nāntarīyakaduḥkhasamedādanatheparaṃparāparicitatvānmūrkhāṇārmākāśodraṅkavadābhāsamānatvādanaikāntikatvādvirodhināṃ yugapadadṛśyamānatvādatyantāvirahitatvācca parihraṇīyaim| ekāntālyantataḥ punarete'bhyupāyāḥ khalu hariharabrahmāṇa iva tulyā eva saṃbhavanti-jñānayoga, prāṇayogo, rasayogaścati | nanu, kathameteṣā tulyatelyapekṣāyā būma:-mokṣeopāye bṛhadvāsiṣṭa bhuśuṇḍopākhyāne vasiṣṭhavākyam-- asādhyaḥ kaścidyugaḥ kasyacijjñānaniścayaḥ | dvau prakāro tato devī jagāda paramaḥ śivaḥ | prāṇānāṃ vā nirodhena vāsanānodanena vā || 125 ||

guruśākhe parityajya vinā jātigandhakāt|
rasaṃ nirmāti durmedhāḥ śapettaṃ parameśvaraḥ || 167 ||
tatra gandhakajāreṇā bahidhūmāntardhumabhedena dvividhā | āādyā yathā-‘tatra ṣaḍguṇagandhakajāraṇakriyā sādhīyasī nigadyate' ityukta prāgeva ||
rasaguṇabalijāraṇaṃ vinā'ya
na khala sujñāṃ hraraṇakṣamo rasendraḥ |
na jaladakalardhītapākahīna:
spṛśati rasāyanatāmiti pratijñā || 168 ||

tannimittakaṃ sikatāyantradvaya kathyate | yathā--niravadhiniṣpīḍitamṛdambarādipariliptāmatikaṭhiṇakācaghaṭīmagre vakṣyamāṇaprakārā rasagarbhiṇīmadhastarjanyaḍulipramāṇitachidrāyāmanurūpa sthālikāyāmāropya chidrasya parito dvitryaṅgulimitena lavaṇena nirantarālīkaraṇapuraḥsara sikatābhirāpūrya vardhamānakamāropaṇīryaḥ kramataśca tricaturāṇi pañcaṣāṇi vā vāsarāṇi jvalanajvālayā pacanīyamityeka yarca; hastaikamātrapramāṇabhūgarbhāntarnikhātā prāguktakācaghaṭī nāticipiṭamukhī nātyuccamukhī maṣībhājanaprāyā khapairacakrikayā kācacakrikayā vā niruddhavadanavivarā mṛṇmayī vā ghaṭī vidhāya karīṣairupari puṣṭo deya ilyanyadyatram | ‘yatramadhye tu bhūdharaḥ' iti trivikramaḥ || ‘kūrmayatre rase gandha ṣaḍguṇaṃ jārayedbudha:' ityanye | atra pakṣe rāgastathā na khāt, tenādau trigurṇa jārayitvā śeṣaḥ kacchapikodare deyaḥ, tadā rāgaḥ sādhuḥ khān | atra kañjalimantareṇa kevalagandhakamapi sāmyena jārayanti |

1 prāgvakācaghaṭī' kha़ |
atha rasasindūrādiṣvanubhūtgndhakajāraṇāprakāramāh| -
kūpīkoṭaramāgarta rasaguṇairgandhaistulāyā vibhuṃ
vijñāya jvalana krameṇa sikatāyace śanai' pācayet |
vāravāramanena vṛṃddhividhinā gandhakṣaye siddhaye
sindūrādyudūito'ṭubhūya bhūṇitaḥ kamekramo'yaṃ mayūā ||
atra sūjhamṛtkapaṭaḥ samudrā kūpaka kṛtvā vālukāmaba yūhara catuṣṭayamajha dadyāt , yathādravya vā punaḥpunarava pāka kuyāt , kūpikāyatraṃ dṛḍha na bhavettadā punarnavīna saṃpādayet, yadi rasasindūra syāttadīdhvaipātanena hiṅgulavanniṣkāsayedikheke | anye'pi gandhakajāraṇaprakārā yatrādhyāye draṣṭavyāḥ | āroṭakamantareṇa hriṅgulagandhakābhyā piṣṭābhyāmapi sindūrarasaḥ sapādyaḥ | āroṭakaśabdastu śuddhaparyāyavācakaḥ ||
anyasaśva-
triguṇamiha rasendramekarmaśa kanakapayodharatārapaṅkajānām |
rasaguṇabalibhirvidhāya piṣṭi racaya nirantaramambubhiḥ kumāryoḥ|
iti kajalīkaraṇam |
āāṣaṅguṇamadharottarasamādibalijāraṇena yojyo'yaṃ yogaḥ |
piṭī pālyū kañjalikārthe jāraṇārthaṃ ca | prakāro'yamadhoyatreṇaiva sidhyati, na punarūdhvaryatreṇeti kajjalī |
kācamṛttikayoḥ kupī hemno'yastārayorapi |
kīlālāya:kṛto lepaḥ khaṭikālavaṇādhikaḥ || 170 ||
anena yantraṣṭritayena bhūri hemābhrasattvānyapi jārayanti |
yathecchamagchai: khamanovicaraiirveicakṣṇāḥ paऴvayantu bhūyaḥ ||171
etatrinetrādiprayogaviṣarya na tu piṣṭīmūrchanaviṣayam |
kṣālayedmlavargeṇa mūrcchitaṃ samalaṃ rasam || 172 | iti

1 *viddhi vidhinā' kha |
antardhūmavipācitaṣaṅguṇagandhena jaritaḥ srutaḥ |
sa bhavetsahasravedhī tāre tābhre bhujaṅge ca || 173 ||
rasaratnākare –
jīrṇe śataguṃṇe gandhe śatavedhī bhavedrasaḥ |
sahasraguṇite jīrṇe sahasrāśena vedhayet || 174 ||
etatprakārastu tatraiva draṣṭavyaḥ; prādhānyaṃ ṣaṅguṇasya sarvesaṃmatam |
atha paṅguṇagandhakajāraṇuyāṃ kacchapayactra yathāākaṇṭhaṃ kalaśa bhūmau nikhāya jalasaṃbhṛtam | śarāvastanmukhe sthāpyo madhye chidrasamanvitaḥ || 175 ||
nīrāviyoginī tatra chidre kācavilepitām |
mṛṇmūṣā sthāpayettakhāmūdhvīdhastulyagandhakam || 176 ||
rasaṃ nikṣipya tasyodhrva śarāveṇa vimudrayet |
vanyopalāmeiṃ tasyodhrva jvālayedgurumāgaitaḥ || 177 ||
khāṅgaśītaṃ samuddhṛtya punasturyāṃśagandhakam |
dattvā pūrvekrameṇaiva jārayet ṣaṅguṇaṃ balim || 178 | iti |
athānyaprakāra: -
sūte gandhaṃ rasaikāśaṃ nikṣipya mṛdukhalvake |
tāvatsaṃkuṭṭayetpiṇḍaṃ bhavedvā tāmrapātrake || 179 ||
tatulyaṃ gandhakaṃ dattvā ruddhūā talṭhohsapuṭe |
puṭyeddhare yatre yāvañjīthti gandhakaḥ | 180 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punagendhaṃ dadyādāṣaṅguṇaṃ budhaḥ || 181 | iti |
atha kacchapayatreṇa gandhakajāraṇamoh śārṅgadharaḥ-
mṛtkuṇḍe prakṣipennīrai tanmadhye ca śarāvakam|
mahatkuṇḍapidhānābha madhye mesvakamyudgam Iા શ૮૨-II

1 ‘samaguṇe' kha' ||

3
litvā ca mekhalāmadhya cūrṇenātra rasa kṣipet |
rasasyopari gandhasya cūrṇaṃ dadyātsamāśakam || 183 ||
dattvopari śarāva ca bhasmamudrā pradāpayet|
tasyopari puṭaṃ dadyācaturbhirgomayopalaiḥ || 184 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punargandhaṃ ṣaṅguṇa jārayedbudhaḥ |
gandhe jīrṇe bhavetsrutastīkṣṇāgneiḥ sarvakāryakṛt||185||iti||
rasaratnākare-
sasthāpya gomaya bhūmau pakamūṣā tathopari |
tanmadhye kaṭutumbyuttha tailaṃ dattvā rasaṃ kṣipet || 186 ||
kākamācīdravo deyatailatulyaḥ punaḥpunaḥ|
gandhaka vrīhimātraṃ ca kṣiptvā tāṃ ca nirodhayet ||187||
(tarrapṛṣṭha śrāvaka deya pūrṇa vā vaṣṭrikharparam |
khāṅgaśītalatāṃ jñātvā jīrṇetailaṃ ca gandhakam |
kākamācīdravaṃ cārmi dattvā dattvā tu jārayet || 188 |)
śrūṣāno gomyaṃ sāndra dṛṣṭvā cordhna cu pāvakṛm|
ṣaṅguṇaṃ gandhake jārya srutakhaiva mukhaṃ bhavet || 189 ||
ityādyajāraṇam |
athānlaya yathāsūtapramṇu sikatākhbayace dattvā baliṃ mṛddhaṭite'lpabhāṇḍe |
telāvaśeṣaṃ ca rasaṃ nidadhyānmajhārdhekāryaṃ pravilokya bhrūyaḥ 190
āṣaṅguṇaṃ gandhakamalpamalpaṃ kṣipedraso jīrṇabalirbalīyān |
raseṣu sarveṣu niyojito'yamasaṃśaya hranti gadaṃ javena | 191 ||
iti gandhakajāraṇam ||

1 aya pāṭha kha pustake nopalabhyate
atha kharṇajāraṇam |
tadukta
gandhakajāraṇamādau kuryādatha jāraṇa suvarṇakha |
jaladharasattvasya tato jāraṇamatha sarvelohānām || 192 ||
atha tadupayogībiḍakathanam | kāñcanādigrasanatīvrabubhukṣākarāṇi dravyāṇi biḍāni kathyante | yathā-
vajrakaṇṭakavajrāgra viddhamaṣṭāṅgula mṛdā |
vilipya goveiśālpāgnau puṭitaṃ tatra śoṣitam || 193 ||
tryahaṃ vajribile kṣipto grāsārthī jāyate rasaḥ |
grasate gandhahemādivajrasattvādeika kṣṇān | 194 ||
mūrchādhyāyoktaṣaṅguṇabalijīrṇepiṣṭikosthitarasaḥ khalve'layantaṃ bubhukṣito ghanahemavajrasattpādei tvaritameva grasatītyanyaprakāraḥ| etadvayamapi kṛtvā vyavahranlyanye | athānye beiḍā:-
satutthaṃ ṭaṅkaṇa sajaiiḥ paṭu tāmre trayaheoṣitam |
kāñjika bhāvita tena gandhādya carati kṣaṇāt || 195 ||
biḍe sakā|blike kṣipto rasaḥ syādvāsalālasa: |
grasate sarvelohāni sarvesattvāni vajrakam || 196 ||
athānyaprakāra: -
śaṅkhacūrṇa ravirdhūtape bhāvayeddinam |
dvañjambīrajadrāvrdineka dhūmasārakam | 197 ||
sauvarcalamajāmūtrairbhīvyaṃ yāmacatuṣṭayam |
kaṇṭakārī ca saṃkāthya dinaika naramūtrake || 198 ||
kharjeikṣāra tuintūiḍīka kāsīsaṃ tu śilājatu |
jambīrotthadravaibhovya pṛthagyāmacatuṣṭayam || 199 ||

nistuṣa jayapāla ca mūlakānā dravairdinam |
saindhavaṃ ṭaṅkaṇa guñjā dinaṃ śiśujaṭāmbhasā | 200 ||
etatsarva samāśa tu madyaṃ jambīrajadravaiḥ |
tadbhola rakṣayedyatnādviḍo'yaṃ vaḍavānalaḥ | 201 ||
anena marditaḥ sūtaḥ saisthitastatkhaṭvake |
khaṇodisarvalohvāni sattvāni grasate kṣaṇāt || 202 ||
iti vaḍavānalo biḍaḥ ||
mūlakārdrakavahīnā kṣāra gomūtragālitay |
vastrapūto dravo grāhyo gandhakaṃ tena bhāvayet |
śatavāraṃ khare gharme biḍo'ya hemajāraṇe || 203 ||
iti siddhalakṣmīśvaratatrokto biḍaḥ ||
evaṃ biḍāntarāṇyapi tatrāntarādanusartavyāni iti biḍakathanam||
brūtūṣaṣṭyaṃśaka hempatraṃ māyūramāyūnā |
vilitaṃ taptakhalvasthe rase dattvā vimardayet |
duinaṃ jambīratoyena grāse grāse tvayaṃ vidhiḥ || 204 ||
śanaiḥ saṃkhedayedbhūrje baddhā sapaṭukāñjike |
bhāṇḍake tridinaṃ sūtaṃ jīṇakharṇa samuddharet || 205 ||
adhikastolitaścetsyātpunaḥ khedyaḥ samāvadhi |
dvātriṃśatṣoḍaśāṣṭāśakrameṇa vasu jārayet || 206 ||
rūpyādeiṣu ca satveṣu vidhirevaṃvidhaḥ smṛtaḥ |
cūllikālavaṇa gandhamabhāve śikhipittataḥ || 207 ||
iti khaṇairūpyādisarvesasvajāraṇavidhiḥ |
atha taptakhalcalakṣaṇam -
ajāśakṛtuṣārdhni ca khātayitvā bhuvi kṣipet |
tasyopari sthito lohkhalvaḥ sa tatkhalvakaḥ | 208 ||
iti taptakhalvalakṣaṇam ||

atha siddhamate dolāyatreṇa hemajāraṇaṃ yathā-
sagrāsaṃ pañcaṣaḍbhāgairdhravakṣrairvimardayet |
mūtaṃ tatṣoḍaśāṃśena gandhenāṣṭāśakena vā || 209 ||
tato vimadya jambīrarase vā kāñjike'thavā |
dolāpāko vidhātavyo dolāyantramid smṛtam || 210 ||
dinatrayaṃ vā jīrṇovadhi dolāpāko vidhātavya iti bhāvaḥ |
iti dolājāraṇam |
athānyatsiddhamate kacchapayatreṇa kāñcanagrarsanaṃ, yathā
śaśvaddhṛtāmbupātrasthaśarāvacchidrasaṃsthitā |
pakamūṣā jale takhā raso'ṣṭāśabiḍāvṛtaḥ || 211 ||
saṃruddho lohapātryā'tha mudrito dṛḍhamudrayā |
vālukā tadupayeṣṭāṅgulamānā vinikṣipet || 212 ||
iṭhāttadupari dhmāto rasastadbharbhasaṃsthitaḥ |
māyūramāyunā liptaṃ kāścana grasati dhruvam || 213 ||
iti kacchapayantram ||
uktaṃ ca-
sacchidraṃ salilāpūrṇabhāṇḍavakre śarāvakam |
dattvā chidre pakamūṣā deyā nīrāviyogini |
tasyā biḍāvṛtaḥ sūto deyo lohāvṛte mukhe || 214 ||
dattvā vālukāmupari mudrā dattvā śanaiḥśanaiḥ |
dhmāto dhmāto grasaleva kāśvane sūkṣmatā gatam || 215 ||
khalpaṃ sapittatāpyātaṃ śanairdarya samāvadhi |
dehrārtha, dhātuvādārtha mayañchantyalpabuddhayaḥ || 216 ||
ityapi kacchapayactram ||

1 "kāñcanakaraṇa' kha !
atha matāntara yathā-
kuṇḍāmbhasi lohamaye sabiḍa sagrāsamīśarja pātre |
aticipiṭalohapātryā pidhāya salipya vadvinā yojya* ||217
iyataiva rasāyanatātparyaṃ paryevasati, keitu vādasya na prādhānyam |
iti hemajāraṇā ||
atha sapratyubhayaprādhānyena jāraṇā yathā, tatra ghanasattvajāraṇam | yata ukta--
ghanarahitabījajāraṇasaprāsadalādisiddhikṛtakṛlyāḥ |
kṛpaṇāḥ prāpya samudra varāṭikālābhasaṃtuṣṭāḥ | 218 ||
muktvaikamabhrasattva nānyo rasapakṣakartanasamarthaḥ |
tena niruddhaprasaro niyamyate badhyate ca sukham || 219 ||
raktai pūītū ca hemārthe kṛṣṇe hemaśarīrayoḥ |
tārakarmeṇi tacchukla kācakiṭṭa sadā tyajet || 220 ||
truṭiśo dattvā mṛdita soṣṇe khalve'bhrasattvahemādi |
carati rasendraḥ kṣitirapamavetasajambīrabījapūrāmlai. || 221
kṣitikhagaḥ kāsīsam | amlābhāve pūrvasādhitakāñjikamapi jñeyam | anyaśca-
abhrakajāraṇamādau gabhedrutijāraṇa ca hemno'nte |
yo jānāti na vādī dṛthaiva so'thekṣya kurute || 222 ||
ityabhrakasattvajāraṇā ||
atha gabhedruti: |
vyomasattvasamaśena tāpyasattvena saiyutam|
sākalyena careddevī gabhedrāvī bhavedrasaḥ || 223 ||

evaṃ hematārābhrādayaḥ khūkharipuṇā nivyūm̐ḍhāḥ prayojanamavalokya prayojyāḥ | garbhadutimantareṇa jaॉraṇaiva na khāt |
atastallakṣaṇamāha-vahniḍayatireke'pi rasagrāsīkṛtānāṃ ( lī hṛānā ) dravatva gabhedrutiḥ |
bījānāṃ saṃskāraḥ kartavyastāṃpyasattvasaṃyogāt |
yena dravanti garbhe rasarājasyāmlaॅvargeṇa || 224 ||
śilayā nihata nāga tāprya vā sindhunā hatam|
tābhyā tu mārita bīja sūtake dravati kṣaṇāt || 225 ||
atha dolājāraṇam |-
paṭūmlakṣāragomūtrajuhīkṣīrapralepite |
bahiśva baddhe vasneṇa bhūrje grāsaniveśitam || 226 ||
kṣārāranālamūtreṣu khedathebridina bhiṣak |
krameṇānena dolāyā jārya grāsacatuṣṭayam |
tataḥ kacchapayagheṇa jvalane jārayedrasam || 227 ||
iti gabhedrutiḥ | atha grāsasya cāraṇapramāṇam |
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakaḥ pūrvo dvātriśāśo dvitīyakaḥ |
tṛtīyaḥ ṣoḍaśāśāstu caturtho'ṣṭāś eva ca || 228 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭyaśakagrāsāddaṇḍadhaiārī bhavedrasaḥ |
jalaukāvadvitīye tu grāsayoge sureśvari || 229 ||
grāsena tu tṛtīyena kākaviṣṭāsamo bhavet |
grāsena tu cūturthena dadhimaṇḍasamo bhavet || 230 ||
anyatu durjestvānna likhitam | bhagavadrovindapādāstu kalāśameva grāsa likhanti | yathā—
pañcabhireva grāsaidhainasattvaṃ jārayitvā''dau |
gabhedrāve nipuṇo jarayati trīrja kalāśena || 231 ||

1 ‘ko'pi tādṛśa prathamam' iti rasahṛdaye pāṭha | 2 *rasarājasyāmlayogena' 3 daṇḍadhārī bhavet vastrānna kṣaratīlyartha |
tanmate catuḥṣaṣṭicatvāriṃśatriṃśadviṃjhatiṣoḍajhāśāḥ paṃca grāsāḥ |
atha rañjanaṃm |
tārakarmaṇyakha na tathā prayogo dṛśyate ||
kevala nirmalaṃ tāmra vāpita daradena tu |
kurute triguṇaṃ jīrṇe lākṣārasanibha rasam || 232 ||
gandhakena hata nāgaṃ jārayetkamalodare |
etasya triguṇe jīrṇe lākṣābho jāyate rasaḥ || 233 ||
kamalodare tāgre | etatu nāgasaṃbadhānna rasāyanakarmaṇi | keivā yathoktasiddhabījopari triguṇatāmrottāraṇadanu tadvījaṃ samajīrṇa san ttreṇaiva rañjayati |
kunaṭīhatakariṇā vā raviṇā vā tāpyagandhakahatena |
daradanihatāsinā vā nivyūḍhaṃ hema taddhījam || 234 ||
balinā vyūḍhaṃ kevalārkamapi ||
kunaṭī manaḥśilā, karināgaḥ, ravistāmraṃ, asileॉhvam |
atha tārabījam--
kuṭilaṃ vimalā tīkṣṇaṃ samaṃ cūrṇa prakalpayet |
puṭitaṃ pañcavāraṃ tu tāre vāhyaṃ śanaidhemet |
yāvaddaśaguṇaṃ tattu tārabījaṃ bhavecchubham || 235 ||
kuṭilaṃ vaṅgaḥ, vimalā kākhamākṣikaṃ, tīkṣṇaṃ lohvam |
sattvaṃ tālodbharve vaṅgaṃ samaṃ kṛtvā tu dhāmayet |
tacūrṇa vāhayettāre guṇān yāvatu ṣoḍaśa || 236 ||
śratibījamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ srutakasya tu bandhanam |
jāraṇātsāraṇācaiva sahsrāṃśena vidhyati || 237 ||

1 ‘rañjanavedhane' ka || 2 ‘triguṇatāmrajāraṇādanu tadbhaya' kha' || 3 ‘khatatreṇaiva' kha | & J āyurvedaprakāśa | 33 vaṅgābhraṃ vāhyettāre guṇāni dvādaśaiva tu | etadbhīje same jīrṇe śatavedhī bhavedrasaḥ || 238 || atha hemabījam nāgābhra vāhayedvaimni dvādaśaiva guṇāni ca | pratibījamid śreṣṭhaṃ pāradasya tu bandhanam || 239 || mākṣikeṇa hata tāmra tāra vā rañjayenmuhuḥ | taṃ nāgaṃ vāhyedbhīje dviṣoḍaśaguṇāni ca || 240 || bījametadvara śreṣṭhaṃ nāgabīja prakīrtitam | samacāritamātreṇa sahasrāṃśena vidhyati || 241 || atha rañjanārthaṃ sāraṇārthaṃ ca tailam - mañjiṣṭhā kiṃśuka caiva khadira raktacandanam | karavīraṃ devadāru saralo rajanīdvayam || 242 || anyāni raktapuṣpāṇi piṣṭrā lākṣārasena tu | tailaṃ vipācayettena kuryādbhījādeirañjanam || 243 || dviguṇaṃ raktapuṣpāṇā raktapītagaṇasya ca | kārtha, caturguṇaṃ kṣīraṃ, tailamekaṃ sureśvari || 244 || jyotiṣmatīkarañjākṣakaṭutumbīsamudbhavam | pāṭalākākatuṇḍyambumahṛārāṣṭrīrasaiḥ pṛthak || 245 || bhekaśūkarameṣāhimatkhakūrmajalaukasām| vasayā caikyā yuktaṃ ṣoḍaśāśaiḥ supeṣitaiḥ || 246 || bhūlatāmalamākṣīkadvandvamelāpakauṣadhaiḥ | pācitaṃ gālitaṃ caiva sāraṇātailamucyate || 247 || atra gandharvatailamapi rasahṛdayakharasāt | atha dvandvamelāpakoṣadhāni–'ūrṇāṭaṅkaṇagirijatumahiṣīkarṇākṣimalaśakragopakarke
